# Copy-paste prompts: characters and backgrounds

One character prompt and one background prompt for every character, ready to paste into DALL·E / ChatGPT image generation. Nothing else is in this file (attributes, lore and art direction live in CAST.md and CAST.xlsx).

**How to use**
- **Character prompt:** paste it as is. Ask for a 3:4 portrait (1024x1536) with a transparent background. If your tool cannot do transparency, generate on flat white or flat green and cut it out. Optional but helpful: attach the approved characters (Interceptors, Owlwing Watch, Moonrunners) as style references.
- **Background prompt:** paste it as is. Ask for a 16:9 landscape (1536x1024).
- The hex colors in each prompt are exact; keep them. Every prompt is under 4000 characters.
- A line under each heading shows the three character colors and the people background color so you can double check the result.

**Contents:** [Human](#human) · [Vampire](#vampire) · [Were-Creature](#were) · [Ghost](#ghost) · [Undead](#undead) · [Forest Spirit](#forest) · [Eldritch](#eldritch) · [Magic](#magic)


---

<a id="human"></a>
# Human


## Human › Roadwar Convoys

### Interceptors

*Combat · Bruiser · colors #d00000 / #f8f9fa / #ffba08 · background wash #d9a521 · art already made*

**Character prompt**

```text
A character model-sheet drawing for a 2D television cartoon in the exact visual style of Samurai Jack (Genndy Tartakovsky): extremely simple large flat shapes, one thin uniform black outline, flat colors, minimal detail, angular geometry, plus Sin City heavy solid black shapes and a few stark white highlights. It must look like a cel from the show, NOT detailed anime or fantasy concept art.
CHARACTER: Interceptors, male human (Irish-Scottish heritage, Scottish), apparent age mid 40s. wiry and hard-jawed, medium size. Pose: wide fighting stance with the crowbar swung back over one shoulder. Expression: fierce, jaw set, one eyebrow cocked, as a minimal face. Design the silhouette first (heavy trapezoids, oversized fists and shoulders); the pose and the hero prop must read as a solid black shape.
SIMPLIFICATION BUDGET (the most important rule): HERO PROP, the one detailed object: ram-bar. SECOND ITEM, kept plain: tire-iron. COSTUME: only three big masses (main garment, legs, boots) as large flat angular polygons. Source costume notes, to be CUT by about 70% and used only as a loose guide: hard-jawed human driver in a spiked black leather jacket, driving gloves, goggles pushed up on the forehead and boots, one hand gripping a heavy tire-iron, the other fist wrapped in a spiked knuckle-duster.

HOW TO DRAW IT: FIGURE: about 12 to 15 big shapes in total; OUTLINE: one thin, uniform-weight black line; SHADING: each material is one flat color plus at most one flat darker shadow shape; no shine or soft shading; FACE: minimal; simple geometric eye shapes under one brow line, one nose line, one mouth line; no eyelashes, eye highlights or skin shading; HAIR and FUR: two to four big solid pointed shapes, never strands, braids, curls or texture; FABRIC: large flat planes with straight or sharply angled edges and at most three big creases; no ragged hems, streamers, patterns, trim bands, tassels, stitching, studs or rows of buckles; METAL and GEAR: plain solid shapes in flat grey, black or an assigned color; no engraving; HANDS and FEET: simple blocky shapes; at most ONE repeated graphic symbol on the whole figure; clear negative space between limbs, prop and body.
COLOR (exact hex, flat fills): main #d00000 about 60%, on the largest garment, armor, fur-marking or body areas; secondary #f8f9fa about 30%, on trim, sash, straps, secondary garment pieces and held gear; pop #ffba08 about 10%, on the eyes, the Armored coupe and one small magical detail. Also allowed: black, white, neutral grey and one flat simplified skin, fur or species tone. No cream, tan, brass, gold or silver unless assigned. Never use #d9a521 (background only).
NEVER: photorealism, 3D or CGI, painterly or watercolor texture, detailed anime rendering, airbrushing, gradients, glow or bloom or lens flare (a glow is a flat solid shape), fabric texture, tiny decorations, individual hairs, realistic reflections, generic fantasy concept art.
SELF-CHECK: it must pass the black-fill, few-shapes, thumbnail, flat-color and distinctive-without-detail tests; if not, remove detail.
COMPOSITION: one full-body figure, head to toe, nothing cropped, clear margin, centered, all shapes fully filled and opaque. True transparent background: no floor, shadow, scene, checkerboard, border or text. 3:4 portrait. Bruiser, combat, Human people, mature dark-age tone, no nudity, no gore.
```

**Background prompt**

```text
Heavily stippled charcoal and ink illustration on toned paper, a graphic-novel panel with Sin City contrast: dense dotted halftone and grainy ink texture over every surface, bold black ink linework, hand-drawn painted shapes. NOT photorealistic, NOT a 3D render, NOT smooth digital concept art, no lens flare. 16:9 landscape, 1980s post-apocalyptic neo-retro cyberpunk dark-age mood.
SCENE: a barricaded highway-overpass settlement: stacked shipping containers, riveted gates, a tall radio mast, sodium street lamps, rain-slick asphalt, tarp banners. An empty environment for Interceptors with no people, creatures, figures, silhouettes or text. Midground: an armored coupe with a spiked ram-bar and one glowing amber headlight parked at the lower-right, stacks of spare tires. Foreground: a fuel drum, a hand-painted convoy banner, plus wet ground reflecting light. Large props the character cannot wear or carry live here, never in a hand.
COLOR (most important): a GOLD-washed painting. The people color #d9a521 washes about 65% of the image across sky, fog, ground, walls and haze. Accents about 20%, clearly visible: main #d00000 for the biggest glowing sources and surfaces, secondary #f8f9fa for medium panels, banners and lights, pop #ffba08 for small sparks and glints, reflected on the ground. Black and white for ink linework, deep shadows and highlights.
COMPOSITION: horizon at one-third height, a moon upper-left, open empty space in the lower center where the character will stand, props pushed to the left and right edges.
```

**Character prompt**

```text
A character model-sheet drawing for a 2D television cartoon in the exact visual style of Samurai Jack (Genndy Tartakovsky): extremely simple large flat shapes, one thin uniform black outline, flat colors, minimal detail, angular geometry, plus Sin City heavy solid black shapes and a few stark white highlights. It must look like a cel from the show, NOT detailed anime or fantasy concept art.
CHARACTER: Necromancers, a hollow-cheeked woman in her sixties of Egyptian heritage, a Mystic who failed the trials and kept the bones, apparent age mid 60s. tall and hollow-cheeked, a hunched staff-leaning stoop, long ink-stained fingers, medium size. Pose: kneeling on one knee with the staff raised in one hand and the other palm pressed down as skeletons rise around her. Expression: calm, hollow-cheeked authority, as a minimal face. Design the silhouette first (one big commanding gesture silhouette with several small, clearly separate minion shapes around it); the pose and the hero prop must read as a solid black shape.
SIMPLIFICATION BUDGET (the most important rule): HERO PROP, the one detailed object: Skull-topped staff. SECOND ITEM, kept plain: hooded cloak. COSTUME: only three big masses (main garment, legs, boots) as large flat angular polygons. Source costume notes, to be CUT by about 70% and used only as a loose guide: tall hooded woman in a long navy robe with teal bone-bead strings, a skull-topped staff raised in one hand, three small skeletons clawing up around her feet, a small bone bird on her shoulder.

SUMMONED MINIONS: the creatures named in the costume notes are part of this image: draw them small (each about a quarter of the hero's height), as simple flat shapes in the same style, clearly separate from the hero and fully inside the canvas.
HOW TO DRAW IT: FIGURE: about 12 to 15 big shapes in total; OUTLINE: one thin, uniform-weight black line; SHADING: each material is one flat color plus at most one flat darker shadow shape; no shine or soft shading; FACE: minimal; simple geometric eye shapes under one brow line, one nose line, one mouth line; no eyelashes, eye highlights or skin shading; HAIR and FUR: two to four big solid pointed shapes, never strands, braids, curls or texture; FABRIC: large flat planes with straight or sharply angled edges and at most three big creases; no ragged hems, streamers, patterns, trim bands, tassels, stitching, studs or rows of buckles; METAL and GEAR: plain solid shapes in flat grey, black or an assigned color; no engraving; HANDS and FEET: simple blocky shapes; at most ONE repeated graphic symbol on the whole figure; clear negative space between limbs, prop and body.
COLOR (exact hex, flat fills): main #134186 about 60%, on the largest garment, armor, fur-marking or body areas; secondary #8ee8c8 about 30%, on trim, sash, straps, secondary garment pieces and held gear; pop #ffcf4a about 10%, on the eyes, the Skull-topped staff and one small magical detail. Also allowed: black, white, neutral grey and one flat simplified skin, fur or species tone. No cream, tan, brass, gold or silver unless assigned. Never use #6b3fa6 (background only).
NEVER: photorealism, 3D or CGI, painterly or watercolor texture, detailed anime rendering, airbrushing, gradients, glow or bloom or lens flare (a glow is a flat solid shape), fabric texture, tiny decorations, individual hairs, realistic reflections, generic fantasy concept art.
SELF-CHECK: it must pass the black-fill, few-shapes, thumbnail, flat-color and distinctive-without-detail tests; if not, remove detail.
COMPOSITION: one full-body figure, head to toe, nothing cropped, clear margin, centered, all shapes fully filled and opaque. True transparent background: no floor, shadow, scene, checkerboard, border or text. 3:4 portrait. Summoner, combat, Magic people, mature dark-age tone, no nudity, no gore.
```

**Background prompt**

```text
Heavily stippled charcoal and ink illustration on toned paper, a graphic-novel panel with Sin City contrast: dense dotted halftone and grainy ink texture over every surface, bold black ink linework, hand-drawn painted shapes. NOT photorealistic, NOT a 3D render, NOT smooth digital concept art, no lens flare. 16:9 landscape, 1980s post-apocalyptic neo-retro cyberpunk dark-age mood.
SCENE: a ruined broadcast-and-ritual precinct: a leaning radio-tower observatory, a mushroom-furred factory floor, a chalk summoning circle on cracked concrete, a broken substation, drifting arcane glow. An empty environment for Necromancers with no people, creatures, figures, silhouettes or text. Midground: a sand-buried sarcophagus hall with open stone coffins, a hieroglyph wall. Foreground: hanging oil lamps, bone piles and a cracked obelisk, plus wet ground reflecting light. Large props the character cannot wear or carry live here, never in a hand.
COLOR (most important): a VIOLET-washed painting. The people color #6b3fa6 washes about 65% of the image across sky, fog, ground, walls and haze. Accents about 20%, clearly visible: main #134186 for the biggest glowing sources and surfaces, secondary #8ee8c8 for medium panels, banners and lights, pop #ffcf4a for small sparks and glints, reflected on the ground. Black and white for ink linework, deep shadows and highlights.
COMPOSITION: horizon at one-third height, a moon upper-left, open empty space in the lower center where the character will stand, props pushed to the left and right edges.
```
